21:T35bb2,<h2 id="overview">Overview<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#overview"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>React 19 introduces a number of new features and improvements that enhance the developer experience, simplify common patterns, and boost performance. Below is a summary along with the key changes and code examples.</p>
<h2 id="whats-new-in-react-19">What’s new in React 19<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#whats-new-in-react-19"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="actions">Actions<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#actions"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>A common use case in React apps is to perform a data mutation and then update the state based on the response.</p>
<p>In the past, pending, state, and optimistic updates had to be managed sequentially as separate pieces of state, but with the introduction of Actions this can now be handled automatically.</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Before Actions</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> UpdateName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({}) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">setName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">setError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">isPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">setIsPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> handleSubmit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setIsPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setIsPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (error) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      setError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(error);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    redirect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/path'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> onChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">event</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(event.target.value)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> onClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">handleSubmit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> disabled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">isPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        Update</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    });</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> onChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">event</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(event.target.value)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> onClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">handleSubmit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> disabled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">isPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<li>
<p>useActionState: Handle common cases for Actions</p>
<ul>
<li>In earlier Canary versions this hook was named useFormState, and it has been renamed to useActionState</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Using &#x3C;form> Actions and useActionState</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ChangeName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">setName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">submitAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">isPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useActionState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">previousState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">formData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> updateName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(formData.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'name'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (error) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> error;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      redirect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/path'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">submitAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"text"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"name"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"submit"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> disabled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">isPending</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        Update</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">error </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<p>React DOM: </p><form> Actions
In React 19 you can pass a function to the action and formAction attributes of , <input>, and <button> elements<p></p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">actionFunction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">+ &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> formAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">formAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Increment&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">+ &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"submit"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> formAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">formAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Increment&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span></code></pre></figure>
</button></form></li>

<li>
<p>useOptimistic: New hook
A hook for optimistically showing the final state while an asynchronous request is in progress</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ChangeName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">currentName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">onUpdateName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">optimisticName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">setOptimisticName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useOptimistic</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(currentName); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// initial state</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> submitAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> formData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> newName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> formData.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'name'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setOptimisticName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(newName); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// set state</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> updatedName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> updateName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(newName);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    onUpdateName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(updatedName);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">submitAction</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Your name is: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">optimisticName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Change Name:&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">          type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"text"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">          name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"name"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">          disabled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">currentName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> optimisticName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>

<h3 id="use">USE<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#use"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>useContext → use</p>
<ul>
<li>The usage is the same as useContext, but the distinguishing feature is that it can also be used inside conditional statements.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { use } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ThemeContext </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './ThemeContext'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Heading</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (children </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // This would not work with useContext</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // because of the early return.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> theme</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(ThemeContext);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> style</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{ color: theme.color }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>promise → use</p>
<ul>
<li>Just wrap the promise with use and wrap the parent with Suspense.</li>
<li>As you can see in the code below, it lets you write concise code, so it looks like it could replace the async libraries you have been using.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { use } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Comments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">commentsPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // `use` will suspend until the promise resolves.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> comments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(commentsPromise);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> comments.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">comment</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">comment.id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">comment</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">commentsPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // When `use` suspends in Comments,</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fallback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Loading...&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Comments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> commentsPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">commentsPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<li>Additional notes
<ul>
<li>How Suspense works
<a href="https://velog.io/@seeh_h/suspense%EC%9D%98-%EB%8F%99%EC%9E%91%EC%9B%90%EB%A6%AC">How Suspense works</a></li>
<li>throw Promise → componetDidCatch(Suspend) → pending, fullfiled, error</li>
</ul>
</li>

<h3 id="react-server-components-and-server-actions">React Server Components and Server Actions<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-server-components-and-server-actions"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>Server Components
An option that lets you pre-render components in an environment separate from the client application or the SSR server.
One common misconception, though: the use server directive is only used in server actions, not in server components.</li>
<li>Server Actions
They let client components call asynchronous functions that execute on the server.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="improvements-in-react-19">Improvements in React 19<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#improvements-in-react-19"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>forwardRef → ref is a prop</p>
<ul>
<li>Changed from the syntax of wrapping with forwardRef to pass ref, so that ref is passed as a regular prop</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> MyInput</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">placeholder</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> placeholder</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">placeholder</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//...</span></span>
