<li>
<p>Cleanup functions for refs
A feature was added that calls a cleanup function on a ref when the component unmounts.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // ref created</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // NEW: return a cleanup function to reset</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // the ref when element is removed from DOM.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // ref cleanup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/></span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>With the introduction of cleanup functions, returning anything else from a ref callback now produces a TypeScript error, so to resolve it you need to make sure the callback does not return a value.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">current</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (instance </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> current)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">current</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      instance </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> current;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>useDeferredValue initial value
It is now possible to set an initial value for useDeferredValue.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Search</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">deferredValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // On initial render the value is ''.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Then a re-render is scheduled with the deferredValue.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useDeferredValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(deferredValue, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Results</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> query</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="support-for-document-metadata">Support for Document Metadata<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#support-for-document-metadata"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>Overview
Built-in support was added for rendering <a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/components/title"><strong><code>&#x3C;title></code></strong></a>, <a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/components/meta"><strong><code>&#x3C;meta></code></strong></a>, and <strong>metadata</strong> <a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/components/link"><strong><code>&#x3C;link></code></strong></a> tags anywhere in the component tree.
It works in both server and client components, meaning that functionality pioneered by libraries such as <strong>React Helmet</strong>, which were already in use, has been brought in-house.
However, if you need to override generic metadata with route-specific metadata based on the current path, you will still need to use a library such as React Helmet.</li>
<li>Example
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> BlogPost</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">post</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">article</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">post.title</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">title</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">post.title</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">title</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">meta</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"author"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"Josh"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"author"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"https://twitter.com/joshcstory/"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">meta</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"keywords"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">post.keywords</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Eee equals em-see-squared...&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">article</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="support-for-stylesheets">Support for stylesheets<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#support-for-stylesheets"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Overview
It provides built-in support for stylesheets, letting you set insertion priority through the precedence style attribute.
Before the client-side rendering commit, it waits for added stylesheets and handles loading them according to their priority.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Example</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ComponentOne</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fallback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"loading..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"stylesheet"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"foo"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> precedence</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"default"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"stylesheet"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"bar"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> precedence</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"high"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">article</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"foo-class bar-class"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        {...}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">article</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ComponentTwo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{...}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"stylesheet"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"baz"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> precedence</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"default"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />  &#x3C;-- will be inserted between foo &#x26; bar</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="support-for-async-scripts">Support for async scripts<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#support-for-async-scripts"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Example</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> MyComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">script</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> src</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      Hello World</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> App</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">html</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">body</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">MyComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">MyComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">> // won't lead to duplicate script in the DOM</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">body</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">html</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="support-for-preloading-resources">Support for preloading resources<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#support-for-preloading-resources"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Overview
A feature was added that tells the browser, both on the initial document load and on client-side updates, about resources that should be loaded as early as possible.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Example</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { prefetchDNS, preconnect, preload, preinit } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-dom'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> MyComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  preinit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://.../path/to/some/script.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {as: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'script'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// loads and executes this script eagerly</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  preload</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://.../path/to/font.woff'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { as: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'font'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// preloads this font</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  preload</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://.../path/to/stylesheet.css'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { as: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'style'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// preloads this stylesheet</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  prefetchDNS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://...'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// when you may not actually request anything from this host</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  preconnect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://...'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// when you will request something but aren't sure what</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// result</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x3C;!--</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> the above would result </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> the following </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">DOM</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">HTML</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> --></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">html</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">head</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;!-- links/scripts are prioritized by their utility to early loading, not call order --></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"prefetch-dns"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"https://..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"preconnect"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"https://..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"preload"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"font"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"https://.../path/to/font.woff"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> rel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"preload"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"style"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"https://.../path/to/stylesheet.css"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">script</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">""</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> src</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"https://.../path/to/some/script.js"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">script</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">head</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">body</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">body</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">html</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/prefetchDNS"><code>prefetchDNS</code></a> : prefetches the IP address of a DNS domain name you intend to connect to.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { prefetchDNS } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-dom'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> AppRoot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  prefetchDNS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://example.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/preconnect"><code>preconnect</code></a> used to connect to a server from which you expect to request resources.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { preconnect } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-dom'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> AppRoot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  preconnect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://example.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/preload"><code>preload</code></a> used to fetch a stylesheet, font, image, or external script that you expect to use.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { preload } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-dom'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> AppRoot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  preload</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://example.com/font.woff2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { as: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'font'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/preloadModule"><code>preloadModule</code></a> lets you fetch an ESM module you intend to use.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { preloadModule } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-dom'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> AppRoot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  preloadModule</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://example.com/module.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { as: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'script'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/preinit"><code>preinit</code></a> used when you want to pre-initialize a script or stylesheet.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { preinit } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-dom'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<h2 id="migration-guide">Migration Guide<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#migration-guide"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>

<h3 id="codemods">Codemods<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#codemods"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> codemod@latest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> react/19/migration-recipe</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<li><a href="https://github.com/reactjs/react-codemod?tab=readme-ov-file#replace-reactdom-render">replace-reactdom-render</a>

</ul>
</li>

</ul>

<h3 id="errors-in-render-are-not-re-thrown">Errors in render are not re-thrown<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#errors-in-render-are-not-re-thrown"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>React 19 introduces new capabilities for fine-grained control over error handling.</p>
<p>You can now adjust how errors are handled using the onCaughtError and onUncaughtError options on the createRoot and hydrateRoot methods.</p>
<p>The existing problem</p>
<p>Previously, even when you caught errors using an Error Boundary, the errors were still printed to the console via console.error.</p>
<p>As a result, developers saw unnecessary error messages even though the errors had been handled properly, which could cause confusion during development.</p>
<p>Improvements in React 19</p>
<p>React 19 provides a way to control error handling more granularly.</p>
<p>Using the onCaughtError and onUncaughtError options of createRoot and hydrateRoot, you can override the default behavior of printing error messages to the console.</p>
<p>onCaughtError is used to handle errors caught by an Error Boundary.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createRoot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(container, {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  onUncaughtError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">errorInfo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // ... log error report(Errors that are not caught by an Error Boundary)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  onCaughtError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">errorInfo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // ... log error report(Errors that are caught by an Error Boundary)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="removed-deprecated-react-apis">Removed deprecated React APIs<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#removed-deprecated-react-apis"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Removed: propTypes and defaultProps for functions
PropTypes was deprecated in April 2017. If you want to keep using type checking, use TypeScript or a type-solution package.
Once ES6 default parameters are supported, defaultProps can also be removed (however, it will continue to be supported for class components).</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// &#x3C; 19</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> PropTypes </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'prop-types'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Heading</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Heading.propTypes </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  text: PropTypes.string,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Heading.defaultProps </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  text: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hello, world!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// >= 19</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// After</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">interface</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Heading</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Hello, world!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Legacy Context using contextTypes and getChildContext
Legacy Context was deprecated in October 2018.
If you still want to keep using context types in class components, use the contextType API instead.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// &#x3C; 19</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> PropTypes </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'prop-types'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Parent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> extends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Component</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  static</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> childContextTypes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    foo: PropTypes.string.isRequired,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  getChildContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>

<li>
<p>Removed: string refs
String refs were deprecated in March 2018.
String refs were supported before ref callbacks, but React 19 removes the string ref approach to make React simpler and easier to understand.
If you happen to have code that uses them, you need to switch to the ref callback approach.
codemod: npx codemod@latest react/19/replace-string-ref</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// &#x3C; 19</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> MyComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> extends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Component</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  componentDidMount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.refs.input.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">focus</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>

<li>
<p>Removed: Module pattern factories
Module pattern factories were deprecated in August 2019.
Since React 19 removes support for Module pattern factories, you need to migrate to regular functions.</p>

<li>
<p>Removed: React.createFactory
createFactory was deprecated in February 2020.
Since it is removed in React 19, you need to migrate to plain JSX.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// &#x3C; 19</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createFactory } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFactory</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

</li>
<li>
<p>Removed: react-test-renderer/shallow
In React 18, react-shallow-renderer was re-exported as react-test-renderer/shallow, but since React 19 removes react-test-renderer/shallow, you need to install the react-shallow-renderer package directly.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npm install react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">shallow</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">renderer </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">--</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">save</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">dev</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ShallowRenderer </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-test-renderer/shallow'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ShallowRenderer </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-shallow-renderer'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Removed deprecated React DOM APIs
Importing the act method has changed from react-dom/test-utils to react. (test-utils removed)
codemod: npx codemod@latest react/19/replace-act-import</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {act} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-dom/test-utils'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {act} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Removed: ReactDOM.render, ReactDOM.hydrate
ReactDOM.render and ReactDOM.hydrate were deprecated in March 2022.
ReactDOM.render → ReactDOM.createRoot
ReactDOM.hydrate → ReactDOM.hydrateRoot
codemod: npx codemod@latest react/19/replace-reactdom-render</p>

<li>
<p>Removed: ReactDOM.findDOMNode
ReactDOM.findDOMNode was deprecated in October 2018.
Since findDOMNode is removed, you need to migrate to DOM Refs.</p>
