<h3 id="new-deprecations">New deprecations<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#new-deprecations"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Deprecated: element.ref
Since React 19 supports ref as a prop, element.ref is no longer supported, and to access it you need to use element.props.ref.
Accessing element.ref triggers a warning
<img src="/blog-assets/2024/12/assets/05/image2.png" alt="image.png"></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Deprecated: react-test-renderer
react-test-renderer → @testing-library/react or @testing-library/react-native</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Notable changes
<a href="https://react.dev/blog/2024/04/25/react-19-upgrade-guide#strict-mode-improvements">StrictMode changes</a>
<a href="https://react.dev/blog/2024/04/25/react-19-upgrade-guide#improvements-to-suspense">Improvements to Suspense</a>
<a href="https://react.dev/blog/2024/04/25/react-19-upgrade-guide#umd-builds-removed">UMD builds removed</a>
<a href="https://react.dev/blog/2024/04/25/react-19-upgrade-guide#libraries-depending-on-react-internals-may-block-upgrades">Libraries depending on React internals may block upgrades</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>TypeScript changes
As React 19 APIs were removed, the types associated with the removed APIs were also removed.
codemod: types-react-codemod</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx types</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">codemod@latest .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">to</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">app</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx types</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">codemod@latest react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-default-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">any</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">props .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">to</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">your</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">ts</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">files</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>ref cleanups required
A feature was added that calls a cleanup function on a ref when the component unmounts.
A feature was added that calls a cleanup function on a ref when the component unmounts.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // ref created</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // NEW: return a cleanup function to reset</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // the ref when element is removed from DOM.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // ref cleanup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/></span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>With the introduction of cleanup functions, returning anything else from a ref callback now produces a TypeScript error, so to resolve it you need to make sure the callback does not return a value.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">current</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (instance </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> current)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">current</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      instance </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> current;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>useRef requires an argument
useRef and createContext were updated to require an argument.
null → RefObject</p>
<ul>
<li>The return type is RefObject<t>, so you cannot directly change the .current property value of the ref object</t></li>
<li>However, since the ref object is a shallow copy, its nested properties can be changed
Otherwise (including undefined)</li>
<li>With MutableRefObject&#x3C;T | undefined>, you can directly change the .current property of the ref object</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// @ts-expect-error: Expected 1 argument but saw none</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">useRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Passes</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">useRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">undefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// @ts-expect-error: Expected 1 argument but saw none</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Passes</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">undefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Cannot assign to 'current' because it is a read-only property</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">ref.current </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Changes to the ReactElement TypeScript type
The ReactElement element type was changed from any to unknown.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Example</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> ReactElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'props'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   ^? Before, was 'any', now 'unknown'</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://react.dev/blog/2024/04/25/react-19-upgrade-guide#the-jsx-namespace-in-typescript">The JSX namespace in TypeScript</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Better useReducer typings
The type inference for useReducer was improved. It moved from the previous approach of specifying types explicitly to an approach that infers types as much as possible.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">useReducer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Reducer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">State</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>>(reducer) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  useReducer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(reducer) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  useReducer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Reducer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">State</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>>((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">state</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> state) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  useReducer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">state</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> State</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> state);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="trouble-shooting">Trouble Shooting<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#trouble-shooting"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Removed: @deprecated - Use <code>typeof React.Children</code> instead.
The ReactChildren type, which had been targeted for gradual removal since React 16.x, has been removed. (Use ReactNode or ReactElement as alternatives.)</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Before</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React, { ReactChildren, Component } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// After</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React, { ReactNode, Component } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Removed: @deprecated - Namespace 'React' has no exported member 'ReactText’
The ReactText type was also removed as of React 18. (Use ReactNode or ReactElement as alternatives.)</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Before</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">badge</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: React.ReactText;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// After</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">badge</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: React.ReactNode;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>ERROR(TypeScript) 'component' cannot be used as a JSX component.(react-redux connect, ConnectedComponent)</p>
<ul>
<li>If a type error occurs, use the useSelector and useDispatch hooks.</li>
<li><a href="https://redux.js.org/usage/migrating-to-modern-redux#modernizing-react-components-with-react-redux">https://redux.js.org/usage/migrating-to-modern-redux#modernizing-react-components-with-react-redux</a></li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Before</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> mapStateToProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">state</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">ownProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    todo: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">selectTodoById</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(state, ownProps.todoId),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    activeTodoId: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">selectActiveTodoId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(state),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> mapDispatchToProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> dispatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    todoDeleted</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> dispatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">todoDeleted</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(id)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    todoToggled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> dispatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">todoToggled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(id)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> connect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">mapStateToProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">mapDispatchToProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">)(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">TodoListItem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// After</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Get the actual `dispatch` function with `useDispatch`</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> dispatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useDispatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Select values from the state with `useSelector`</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> activeTodoId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useSelector</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(selectActiveTodoId);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Use prop in scope to select a specific value</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> todo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useSelector</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">state</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> selectTodoById</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(state, todoId));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>ERROR(TypeScript) Property 'className' does not exist on type 'unknown'.
Changes to the ReactElement TypeScript type</p>
<ul>
<li>Since the ReactElement element type was changed from any to unknown, you must explicitly specify the type in order to access it.
Because the component type is React.ReactNode, which is a general-purpose type, specifying the props or className type can cause an error, so you need to use a type assertion to specify the type of that component.</li>
</ul>

<li>
<p>ERROR(TypeScript) Argument of type '({ children, container, disablePortal, onRendered, }: Props, ref: ForwardedRef&#x3C;ReactElement&#x3C;any, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>>)
This issue occurs because the return value is being interpreted as ReactNode, which does not match the ForwardRefRenderFunction type, so you need to explicitly specify the ReactElement type.</any></p>

</ul>
<h2 id="react-compiler">REACT Compiler<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-compiler"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Overview</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://ko.react.dev/learn/react-compiler">https://ko.react.dev/learn/react-compiler</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>What does the compiler do?</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://ko.react.dev/learn/react-compiler#what-does-the-compiler-do">https://ko.react.dev/learn/react-compiler#what-does-the-compiler-do</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Initial assumptions of the compiler</p>
<ul>
<li>Write correct and semantically meaningful JavaScript code</li>
<li>Test whether nullable/optional values and properties are defined before accessing them
<ul>
<li>If you use TypeScript, enable strictNullChecks and then proceed</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Follow the Rules of React properly</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Compatibility check</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> react-compiler-healthcheck@experimental</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Successfully</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> compiled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> out</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 9</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> components.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> Check</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> the</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> components</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> that</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> can</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> be</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> optimized</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> successfully:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> the</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> higher</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> the</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> number,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> the</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> better</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">StrictMode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> usage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> not</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> found.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> Check</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> whether</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">StrictMod</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> is</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> used:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> you</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> enable</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> and</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> follow</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> it,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> you</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> are</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> more</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> likely</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> to</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> be</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> following</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> the</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> Rules</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> well.</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Found</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> no</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> usage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> incompatible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> libraries.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> Check</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> usage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> incompatible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> libraries:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> checks</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> known</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> libraries</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> any</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> that</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> are</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> incompatible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> with</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> the</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> compiler.</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>eslint-plugin-react-compiler (applied to an existing codebase)
Flags violations of the Rules of React
When using compilationMode: "annotation” mode, compilation only proceeds for components that add the “use memo” directive.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> Compatibility</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> check</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> react-compiler-healthcheck@experimental</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> .eslint</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">module.exports</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  plugins:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">    'eslint-plugin-react-compiler'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  rules:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">    'react-compiler/react-compiler'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> "error",</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ReactCompilerConfig</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  compilationMode:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> "annotation",</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> src/app.jsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> default function App() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  "use memo"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  //</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>babel-plugin-react-compiler@experimental (applied to the entire codebase)
babel-plugin-react-compiler must run before other Babel plugins.</p>
